Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the clusters structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 357. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 496 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to clusters structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 357/496 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)
